# Seven boundary points: the complete CD/GH corner-line class

Consider the single-exterior placement of N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md. If the
line joining the two heptagon-interior points crosses the literal hull
edges CD and GH, the minimum ordinary triangle area is strictly below
93/2000<A*. This excludes the entire edge-pair class at every height.

The proof has eighteen fixed cubic rows. They contain **5,926 printed
positive integer terms**. Row R05c also uses explicitly printed
squares of rational linear forms. These squares are nonnegative for all
real coordinates and introduce no additional geometric premise. Every
factor and every sign used by the rows is defined below. The optional
standard-library checker reads the manuscript; no search, optimizer,
coordinate sample or unprinted certificate is a premise.

All areas and determinants below are doubled. Write s=93/1000 and
K=149/20<Gamma2=4+2sqrt(3), and suppose every actual triangle has unsigned
area at least s.

## 1. The containing quadrilateral and its separated diagonals

Use the strict convex actual octagon

    A=(0,a), B=(b,0), C=(c,0), D=(1,d),
    E=(1,e), F=(f,1), G=(g,z), H=(0,h)

and heptagon-interior O=(x,y), P=(u,v), where

    0<a<h<1, 0<b<c<1, 0<d<e<1,
    b<=f<=c, d<=h, a<=e, 0<g<f, h<z<1.

G is strictly above HF. All cyclic octagon triangles have positive
signed area. Orient OP from its intersection with GH toward its
intersection with CD, and label O before P in that direction. Since
every point of the open GH edge has abscissa less than c and ordinate
greater than d, this gives

    U=u-x>0, L=y-v>0,
    DOP,EOP,FOP,GOP>0; AOP,BOP,COP,HOP<0.            (1)

The chord between these two edge intersections lies in the actual
quadrilateral CDGH. Hence both O,P lie strictly inside CDGH.

Each of CDG,CGH,CDH,DGH has doubled area less than 69/100<Gamma2*s,
by N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md. Each therefore contains at most one other
actual point. Both diagonals CG and DH separate O,P. The exact identity

    IJP-IJO=IOP-JOP

and (1) fix the orientations:

    CGO>0>CGP,             DHO<0<DHP.               (2)

The positive diagonal differences are

    M1=CGO-CGP=zU-(c-g)L>=2s,
    M2=DHP-DHO=L-(h-d)U>=2s.                        (3)

Since c>g, h>=d, U,L>0 and z<=1, these imply U,L>=2s.
The doubled area of the containing quadrilateral is

    W0=z-(c-g)(h-d)<=1.                              (4)

QUADRILATERAL_SPREAD.md applies with normalized threshold s/W0>=s>1/11.
The two resulting nonnegative factors are

    SCDGH_CG=W0-6s-M1,       SCDGH_DH=W0-6s-M2.      (5)

No comparison between h and e is assumed.

negative signs. All sixteen words occur below. Only the word
ADP+,BGO-,CFP+,EHO+ needs refinement: first split AFO; its positive
branch is then split by BEP. These are actual triangles, so none has
zero signed area. The resulting eighteen rows are exhaustive.

The additional signs for each row are in the following table. They
follow by these finite rules, all established in the preceding N7
manuscripts.

1. Every cyclic triangle of the eight hull vertices is positive. Each
   edge test for the containing heptagon ABCDEFH and quadrilateral CDGH
   is positive for O,P. Use the four diagonal signs (2).
2. A four-consecutive-vertex cap has capacity one. If its cyclic
   endpoint chord is IJ, its cap is on the negative side of IJ. If
   IOP<0<JOP, then IJO>0; if IOP>0>JOP, then IJP>0.
   Otherwise monotonicity along OP would put both points in that cap.
3. For every hull chord IJ, IJP-IJO=IOP-JOP propagates a known sign in
   the indicated increasing or decreasing direction.
4. Three positive edge tests put a point inside an actual hull
   triangle. Any chord half-plane containing that triangle contains
   the point, by strict barycentric interpolation.
5. Every consecutive triple, every consecutive four-vertex cap, and
   each of AEF,BEF,ABE,ABF,CDG,CGH,CDH,DGH has capacity one. If one
   point lies inside and all but one edge tests for the other point
   are positive, the remaining edge test is negative.
6. For T=O,P the exact identities

       z BHT=h BGT-y_T[b(z-h)+hg],
       DFT=(1-d)CFT+[1-c+d(c-f)](1-y_T)

   give BGT<0 => BHT<0 and CFT>0 => DFT>0.

| Row | Additional positive actual triangles | Additional negative actual triangles |

| R06 | ADP, AEO, BDP, CEP, CFP | AGO, BGO, EHO |

Together with the cyclic hull signs and (1), these tables contain every

reconstructs the tables by the displayed geometric rules.

## 3. Common rational box and affine factors

Put

    eta=93/790, ell=7347/69700, Fmax=1-ell,
    h0=4s/Fmax, h1=1-s, f0=4s/(1-eta), x0=s/(h1-eta).

All eighteen rows use the same closed box in the coordinate order
(a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h,z,x,y,u,v):

| Coordinate | Lower endpoint | Upper endpoint |
|---|---|---|
| a | eta | 1-4s |
| b | x0 | Fmax |
| c | ell | Fmax |
| d | eta | h1 |
| e | h0 | 1 |
| f | f0 | Fmax |
| g | x0 | 3/5 |
| h | h0 | h1 |
| z | h0+s | 1 |
| x | x0 | Fmax-2s |
| y | eta+2s | 1-s |
| u | x0+2s | Fmax |
| v | eta | 1-3s |

These are the ranges in N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md, with g<3/5 from
N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md, and the four refinements from U,L>=2s.
For completeness, AHT=-(h-a)x_T and h-a<=h1-eta give x_T>=x0.
The upper ordinate bound follows from the two actual edge clearances
FHT,EFT and

    (1-e)FHT+(1-h)EFT=EFH(1-y_T),
    EFH=(1-f)(1-h)+f(1-e)<=2-e-h.

They give y_T<=1-s. The other initial bounds are the published
single-exterior ranges. Closing strict endpoints enlarges the domain.

For coordinate j, j=0,...,12, let L_(2j) be the coordinate minus its
lower endpoint, and L_(2j+1) its upper endpoint minus the coordinate.
The remaining affine factors, in exact index order, are:

| Index | Factor | Index | Factor |
|---:|---|---:|---|
| 26 | h-a-s | 27 | 697/790-h+a |
| 28 | e-d-s | 29 | 697/790-e+d |
| 30 | c-b-117/1000 | 31 | 79/100-c+b |
| 32 | f-b | 33 | c-f |
| 34 | f-g | 35 | h-d |
| 36 | e-a | 37 | z-h-s |
| 38 | u-x-2s | 39 | y-v-2s |

All are nonnegative by the normalization, the published ranges and (3).

## 4. Support, cap, occupancy and five-chain factors

The following factors retain their already proved definitions:

    EP=f(1-a)-4s, EL=hc-4s, ER=e(1-b)-4s,
    SUP=f^2(z-a)-fg(1-a)-4s(f-g),
    P_AHGFE=f(1-e)+(1-f)(1-a-11s),
    P_BCDEF=e(1-f)+(1-e)(1-b-11s),
    P_DEFGH=g(h-d)+z-h-11sg,
    P_HABCD=(h-11s)(1-c)+dc,
    P_ABCDE=(e-11s)b+a(1-b),
    UBR=9s-e(1-b),
    UTR=9s(f-g)-(1-g)[(1-d)(f-g)+(1-z)(1-f)],
    PAIR_BL=hc-ABC-ABH-2s,
    PAIR_BR=e(1-b)-BCD-CDE-2s.

The endpoint supports, paired-ear estimates and upper corner containers
are proved in N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md and N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md. For
example sqrt(ABC)+sqrt(ABH)<=sqrt(hc), together with ABC,ABH>=s,
gives PAIR_BL>=0. The right-side proof is identical.

For W=ABCD,CDEF,EFGH,GHAB, UW means 8s-area(W).
For T=AEF,BEF,ABE,ABF,CDG,CGH,CDH,DGH, UT means 69/100-T.
For T=ABC,ABH,BCD,CDE,AGH,FGH, EAR_T means 121/250-T.
All are nonnegative by the published metric cap and long-triangle bounds.

A triangle factor CT_1 or CT_2 means area(T)-3s or area(T)-Ks.
Its indicated one or two actual occupants are listed below. For an
actual convex quadrilateral containing O,P, QW means area(W)-8s.
If both diagonals also separate O,P, RW_IJ means

    area(W)-(29/5)s-epsilon*(IJO-IJP),                (6)

where epsilon is the sign of IJO. This is the threshold-free spread
bound, since 29/5<3+2sqrt(2). The factor SW_IJ instead uses 6 in place
of 29/5, and is used only for W=ABEF or CDGH, whose area is at most one.
The CDGH instances are exactly (5).

The new notation Q1_W means area(W)-(24/5)s. Here W is any positively
ordered actual convex quadrilateral, possibly with O or P as a vertex,
and it contains the other actual point identified in the table.
To justify the cost, let its four fan areas around that point be
a0,b0,c0,d0. Every fan area is at least s, and the determinant identity
for the two actual diagonal triangles gives
|a0*c0-b0*d0|>=s^2. After interchanging the products,
a0*c0>=b0*d0+s^2>=2s^2. Therefore

    area(W)>=2sqrt(a0*c0)+2sqrt(b0*d0)
            >=2(1+sqrt(2))s>(24/5)s.

This is the one-interior-point quadrilateral proof recorded in
U3_FOUR_ONE_SIDE.md, Section 3.

For five actual hull vertices A0,B0,C0,D0,E0 in cyclic order, put

    X=A0B0E0, Y=A0D0E0, Z0=A0B0D0.

The universal five-chain support argument of
N7_THREE_FIVE_UPPER_HEIGHT.md proves XY>=11s(X-Z0). If X-Z0<=0
this is immediate. Otherwise the supporting rays A0B0 and E0D0
meet beyond both contacts, and their endpoint triangle has area
XY/(X-Z0)>11s. If X<=B or Y<=B, respectively, this gives the
nonnegative quadratic factors

    F5_A0B0C0D0E0_X = B*Y-11s*(X-Z0),
    F5_A0B0C0D0E0_Y = 11s*Z0-(11s-B)*X.             (7)

Every used B is in the next table. It follows by containment in one of
the four-consecutive caps, an ear triangle, or a long triangle. The
available upper bounds are 8s for even-starting caps
ABCD,CDEF,EFGH,GHAB; (1-s)^2 for HABC,FGHA; 9s for BCDE,DEFG;
69/100 for the eight long triangles; 121/250 for the six ears above;
and 1-4s for DEF,EFG. Taking the minimum of applicable bounds is valid.

## 5. Explicit square factors

The following table defines every SQ factor. Its columns after the
name and positive denominator are the integer coefficients of
(1,a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h,z,x,y,u,v). If that row is
(name,denom,c0,...,c13), its factor is

    name=[(c0+c1*a+c2*b+...+c13*v)/denom]^2.          (8)

Every factor is nonnegative on all real coordinates. Its product with
any nonnegative affine L factor has degree at most three. No sign
or equality involving the linear form is assumed.

## 6. Exact contradictions and complete scope

Each row's printed polynomial W is a positive integer sum of products
of the stated nonnegative factors. Thus W>=0. Expand it as
sum(c_alpha X^alpha). The common positive rational box gives the exact
upper bound

    B(W)=sum_(c_alpha>0)c_alpha product_j(upper_j^alpha_j)
         +sum_(c_alpha<0)c_alpha product_j(lower_j^alpha_j).

Every value in the next table is strictly negative. The eighteen rows
cover all sixteen oblique words, including both AFO signs and both BEP
signs in their specified parent branches. This contradiction proves
the whole literal CD/GH edge-pair class has an actual triangle with
doubled area below s.

This statement does not rotate the missing top contact or identify
distinct seven-contact normalizations. The other edge-pair classes
and the all-interior N7 placement remain separate global obligations.

## 7. Complete fixed integer sums

### Row R01

The exact bound is `-63071990870146331816439731579313/1148449295261032038168301562500`.

| Index | Weight | Product |
